#dinar
introduction_of_dinar = {
	if = {
		limit = { #Introduction of dinar
			currency_identifiers_dinar = 1
		}
		if = { #Silver standart
			limit = {
				has_law = law_type:law_fiat_standard
				game_date < 1873.1.1
			}

			activate_law = law_type:law_silver_standard

			if = {
				limit = { exists = capital }
				capital = {
					save_scope_as = central_bank_site
				}
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = money_value_target_1
				value = { 15 30 } #{ 1 12 }
			}
			if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = circulating_dinar_c_var_1 } } set_variable = { name = circulating_dinar_c_var_1 value = 0 } }
			change_variable = {
				name = circulating_dinar_c_var_1
				add = { 50000009 100000009 }
			}
			scope:central_bank_site ?= {
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = stockpiling_dinar_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_dinar_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = stockpiling_dinar_state_1
					add  = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_dinar_c_var_1
						multiply = 0.101
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = silver_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = silver_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = silver_state_1
					add = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_dinar_c_var_1
						add = var:stockpiling_dinar_state_1
                        add = owner.building_saving
						multiply = owner.var:money_value_target_1
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = gold_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = gold_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = gold_state_1
					add = {
						value = var:silver_state_1
						multiply = silver_to_gold_rate
					}
				}
			}

			#Event new central bank and new currency
			if = {
				limit = {
					not = {
						has_variable = country_already_central_bank
					}
				}
				set_variable = country_already_central_bank

				if = {
					limit = { exists = capital }
					capital= {
						save_scope_as = capital_state
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = target_country

				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_player = yes
					}
					trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.1
				}
			}

		}
		if = { #Gold standart
			limit = {
				has_law = law_type:law_fiat_standard
				game_date > 1873.1.1
			}

			activate_law = law_type:law_gold_standard

			if = {
				limit = { exists = capital }
				capital = {
					save_scope_as = central_bank_site
				}
			}

			set_variable = {
				name = money_value_target_1
				value = { 1 2 }
			}
			if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = circulating_dinar_c_var_1 } } set_variable = { name = circulating_dinar_c_var_1 value = 0 } }
			change_variable = {
				name = circulating_dinar_c_var_1
				add = { 50000009 100000009 }
			}
			scope:central_bank_site ?= {
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = stockpiling_dinar_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_dinar_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = stockpiling_dinar_state_1
					add  = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_dinar_c_var_1
						multiply = 0.101
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = gold_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = gold_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = gold_state_1
					add = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_dinar_c_var_1
						add = var:stockpiling_dinar_state_1
						add = owner.building_saving
						multiply = owner.var:money_value_target_1
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = silver_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = silver_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = silver_state_1
					add =  var:gold_state_1
				}
			}

			#Event new central bank and new currency
			if = {
				limit = {
					not = {
						has_variable = country_already_central_bank
					}
				}
				set_variable = country_already_central_bank

				if = {
					limit = { exists = capital }
					capital= {
						save_scope_as = capital_state
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = target_country

				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_player = yes
					}
					trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.1
				}
			}

		}
		set_variable = { name = zz_ef_cur value = flag:dinar }
		zz_ef_cur_name_set = yes
	}
}

					add = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_dinar_moroccan_dirham_c_var_1
						add = var:stockpiling_dinar_moroccan_dirham_state_1
                        add = owner.building_saving
						multiply = owner.var:money_value_target_1
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = gold_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = gold_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = gold_state_1
					add = {
						value = var:silver_state_1
						multiply = silver_to_gold_rate
					}
				}
			}

			#Event new central bank and new currency
			if = {
				limit = {
					not = {
						has_variable = country_already_central_bank
					}
				}
				set_variable = country_already_central_bank

				if = {
					limit = { exists = capital }
					capital= {
						save_scope_as = capital_state
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = target_country

				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_player = yes
					}
					trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.1
				}
			}

		}
		if = { #Gold standart
			limit = {
				has_law = law_type:law_fiat_standard
				game_date > 1873.1.1
			}

			activate_law = law_type:law_gold_standard

			if = {
				limit = { exists = capital }
				capital = {
					save_scope_as = central_bank_site
				}
			}

			set_variable = {
				name = money_value_target_1
				value = { 1 2 }
			}
			if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = circulating_dinar_moroccan_dirham_c_var_1 } } set_variable = { name = circulating_dinar_moroccan_dirham_c_var_1 value = 0 } }
			change_variable = {
				name = circulating_dinar_moroccan_dirham_c_var_1
				add = { 50000009 100000009 }
			}
			scope:central_bank_site ?= {
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = stockpiling_dinar_moroccan_dirham_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_dinar_moroccan_dirham_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = stockpiling_dinar_moroccan_dirham_state_1
					add  = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_dinar_moroccan_dirham_c_var_1
						multiply = 0.101
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = gold_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = gold_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = gold_state_1
					add = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_dinar_moroccan_dirham_c_var_1
						add = var:stockpiling_dinar_moroccan_dirham_state_1
						add = owner.building_saving
						multiply = owner.var:money_value_target_1
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = silver_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = silver_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = silver_state_1
					add =  var:gold_state_1
				}
			}

			#Event new central bank and new currency
			if = {
				limit = {
					not = {
						has_variable = country_already_central_bank
					}
				}
				set_variable = country_already_central_bank

				if = {
					limit = { exists = capital }
					capital= {
						save_scope_as = capital_state
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = target_country

				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_player = yes
					}
					trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.1
				}
			}

		}
		set_variable = { name = zz_ef_cur value = flag:dinar_moroccan_dirham }
		zz_ef_cur_name_set = yes
	}
}
#dinar_omanian_rial
introduction_of_dinar_omanian_rial = {
	if = {
		limit = { #Introduction of dinar_omanian_rial
			currency_identifiers_dinar_omanian_rial = 1
		}
		if = { #Silver standart
			limit = {
				has_law = law_type:law_fiat_standard
				game_date < 1873.1.1
			}

			activate_law = law_type:law_silver_standard

			if = {
				limit = { exists = capital }
				capital = {
					save_scope_as = central_bank_site
				}
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = money_value_target_1
				value = { 15 30 } #{ 1 12 }
			}
			if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = circulating_dinar_omanian_rial_c_var_1 } } set_variable = { name = circulating_dinar_omanian_rial_c_var_1 value = 0 } }
			change_variable = {
				name = circulating_dinar_omanian_rial_c_var_1
				add = { 50000009 100000009 }
			}
			scope:central_bank_site ?= {
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = stockpiling_dinar_omanian_rial_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_dinar_omanian_rial_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = stockpiling_dinar_omanian_rial_state_1
					add  = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_dinar_omanian_rial_c_var_1
						multiply = 0.101
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = silver_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = silver_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = silver_state_1
					add = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_dinar_omanian_rial_c_var_1
						add = var:stockpiling_dinar_omanian_rial_state_1
                        add = owner.building_saving
						multiply = owner.var:money_value_target_1
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = gold_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = gold_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = gold_state_1
					add = {
						value = var:silver_state_1
						multiply = silver_to_gold_rate
					}
				}
			}

			#Event new central bank and new currency
			if = {
				limit = {
					not = {
						has_variable = country_already_central_bank
					}
				}
				set_variable = country_already_central_bank

				if = {
					limit = { exists = capital }
					capital= {
						save_scope_as = capital_state
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = target_country

				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_player = yes
					}
					trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.1
				}
			}

		}
		if = { #Gold standart
			limit = {
				has_law = law_type:law_fiat_standard
				game_date > 1873.1.1
			}

			activate_law = law_type:law_gold_standard

			if = {
				limit = { exists = capital }
				capital = {
					save_scope_as = central_bank_site
				}
			}

			set_variable = {
				name = money_value_target_1
				value = { 1 2 }
			}
			if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = circulating_dinar_omanian_rial_c_var_1 } } set_variable = { name = circulating_dinar_omanian_rial_c_var_1 value = 0 } }
			change_variable = {

						multiply = 0.101

			if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = circulating_dinar_serbian_dinar_c_var_1 } } set_variable = { name = circulating_dinar_serbian_dinar_c_var_1 value = 0 } }
			change_variable = {
				name = circulating_dinar_serbian_dinar_c_var_1
				add = { 50000009 100000009 }
			}
			scope:central_bank_site ?= {
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = stockpiling_dinar_serbian_dinar_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_dinar_serbian_dinar_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = stockpiling_dinar_serbian_dinar_state_1
					add  = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_dinar_serbian_dinar_c_var_1
						multiply = 0.101
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = gold_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = gold_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = gold_state_1
					add = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_dinar_serbian_dinar_c_var_1
						add = var:stockpiling_dinar_serbian_dinar_state_1
						add = owner.building_saving
						multiply = owner.var:money_value_target_1
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = silver_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = silver_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = silver_state_1
					add =  var:gold_state_1
				}
			}

			#Event new central bank and new currency
			if = {
				limit = {
					not = {
						has_variable = country_already_central_bank
					}
				}
				set_variable = country_already_central_bank

				if = {
					limit = { exists = capital }
					capital= {
						save_scope_as = capital_state
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = target_country

				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_player = yes
					}
					trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.1
				}
			}

		}
		set_variable = { name = zz_ef_cur value = flag:dinar_serbian_dinar }
		zz_ef_cur_name_set = yes
	}
}
#dinar_tunisian_dinar
introduction_of_dinar_tunisian_dinar = {
	if = {
		limit = { #Introduction of dinar_tunisian_dinar
			currency_identifiers_dinar_tunisian_dinar = 1
		}
		if = { #Silver standart
			limit = {
				has_law = law_type:law_fiat_standard
				game_date < 1873.1.1
			}

			activate_law = law_type:law_silver_standard

			if = {
				limit = { exists = capital }
				capital = {
					save_scope_as = central_bank_site
				}
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = money_value_target_1
				value = { 15 30 } #{ 1 12 }
			}
			if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = circulating_dinar_tunisian_dinar_c_var_1 } } set_variable = { name = circulating_dinar_tunisian_dinar_c_var_1 value = 0 } }
			change_variable = {
				name = circulating_dinar_tunisian_dinar_c_var_1
				add = { 50000009 100000009 }
			}
			scope:central_bank_site ?= {
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = stockpiling_dinar_tunisian_dinar_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_dinar_tunisian_dinar_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = stockpiling_dinar_tunisian_dinar_state_1
					add  = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_dinar_tunisian_dinar_c_var_1
						multiply = 0.101
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = silver_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = silver_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = silver_state_1
					add = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_dinar_tunisian_dinar_c_var_1
						add = var:stockpiling_dinar_tunisian_dinar_state_1
                        add = owner.building_saving
						multiply = owner.var:money_value_target_1
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = gold_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = gold_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = gold_state_1
					add = {
						value = var:silver_state_1
						multiply = silver_to_gold_rate
					}
				}
			}

			#Event new central bank and new currency
			if = {
				limit = {
					not = {
						has_variable = country_already_central_bank
					}
				}
				set_variable = country_already_central_bank

				if = {
					limit = { exists = capital }
					capital= {
						save_scope_as = capital_state
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = target_country

				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_player = yes
					}
					trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.1
				}
			}

		}
		if = { #Gold standart
			limit = {
				has_law = law_type:law_fiat_standard
				game_date > 1873.1.1
			}

			activate_law = law_type:law_gold_standard

			if = {
				limit = { exists = capital }
				capital = {
					save_scope_as = central_bank_site
				}
			}

			set_variable = {
				name = money_value_target_1
				value = { 1 2 }
			}
			if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = circulating_dinar_tunisian_dinar_c_var_1 } } set_variable = { name = circulating_dinar_tunisian_dinar_c_var_1 value = 0 } }
			change_variable = {
				name = circulating_dinar_tunisian_dinar_c_var_1
				add = { 50000009 100000009 }
			}
			scope:central_bank_site ?= {
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = stockpiling_dinar_tunisian_dinar_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_dinar_tunisian_dinar_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = stockpiling_dinar_tunisian_dinar_state_1
					add  = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_dinar_tunisian_dinar_c_var_1
						multiply = 0.101
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = gold_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = gold_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = gold_state_1
					add = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_dinar_tunisian_dinar_c_var_1
						add = var:stockpiling_dinar_tunisian_dinar_state_1
						add = owner.building_saving
						multiply = owner.var:money_value_target_1
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = silver_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = silver_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = silver_state_1
					add =  var:gold_state_1
				}

					}

			}

		}

				limit = {
					not = {
						has_variable = country_already_central_bank
					}
				}
				set_variable = country_already_central_bank

				if = {
					limit = { exists = capital }

					}
				}
				save_scope_as = target_country

				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_player = yes
					}
					trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.1
				}
			}

		}
		set_variable = { name = zz_ef_cur value = flag:dollar_canadian_dollar }
		zz_ef_cur_name_set = yes
	}
}
#dollar_caribbean_dollar
introduction_of_dollar_caribbean_dollar = {
	if = {
		limit = { #Introduction of dollar_caribbean_dollar
			currency_identifiers_dollar_caribbean_dollar = 1
		}
		if = { #Silver standart
			limit = {
				has_law = law_type:law_fiat_standard
				game_date < 1873.1.1
			}

			activate_law = law_type:law_silver_standard

			if = {
				limit = { exists = capital }
				capital = {
					save_scope_as = central_bank_site
				}
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = money_value_target_1
				value = { 15 30 } #{ 1 12 }
			}
			if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = circulating_dollar_caribbean_dollar_c_var_1 } } set_variable = { name = circulating_dollar_caribbean_dollar_c_var_1 value = 0 } }
			change_variable = {
				name = circulating_dollar_caribbean_dollar_c_var_1
				add = { 50000009 100000009 }
			}
			scope:central_bank_site ?= {
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = stockpiling_dollar_caribbean_dollar_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_dollar_caribbean_dollar_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = stockpiling_dollar_caribbean_dollar_state_1
					add  = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_dollar_caribbean_dollar_c_var_1
						multiply = 0.101
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = silver_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = silver_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = silver_state_1
					add = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_dollar_caribbean_dollar_c_var_1
						add = var:stockpiling_dollar_caribbean_dollar_state_1
                        add = owner.building_saving
						multiply = owner.var:money_value_target_1
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = gold_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = gold_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = gold_state_1
					add = {
						value = var:silver_state_1
						multiply = silver_to_gold_rate
					}
				}
			}

			#Event new central bank and new currency
			if = {
				limit = {
					not = {
						has_variable = country_already_central_bank
					}
				}
				set_variable = country_already_central_bank

				if = {
					limit = { exists = capital }
					capital= {
						save_scope_as = capital_state
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = target_country

				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_player = yes
					}
					trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.1
				}
			}

		}
		if = { #Gold standart
			limit = {
				has_law = law_type:law_fiat_standard
				game_date > 1873.1.1
			}

			activate_law = law_type:law_gold_standard

			if = {
				limit = { exists = capital }
				capital = {
					save_scope_as = central_bank_site
				}
			}

			set_variable = {
				name = money_value_target_1
				value = { 1 2 }
			}
			if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = circulating_dollar_caribbean_dollar_c_var_1 } } set_variable = { name = circulating_dollar_caribbean_dollar_c_var_1 value = 0 } }
			change_variable = {
				name = circulating_dollar_caribbean_dollar_c_var_1
				add = { 50000009 100000009 }
			}
			scope:central_bank_site ?= {
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = stockpiling_dollar_caribbean_dollar_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_dollar_caribbean_dollar_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = stockpiling_dollar_caribbean_dollar_state_1
					add  = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_dollar_caribbean_dollar_c_var_1
						multiply = 0.101
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = gold_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = gold_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = gold_state_1
					add = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_dollar_caribbean_dollar_c_var_1
						add = var:stockpiling_dollar_caribbean_dollar_state_1
						add = owner.building_saving
						multiply = owner.var:money_value_target_1
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = silver_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = silver_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = silver_state_1
					add =  var:gold_state_1
				}
			}

			#Event new central bank and new currency
			if = {
				limit = {
					not = {
						has_variable = country_already_central_bank
					}
				}
				set_variable = country_already_central_bank

				if = {
					limit = { exists = capital }
					capital= {
						save_scope_as = capital_state
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = target_country

				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_player = yes
					}
					trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.1
				}
			}

		}
		set_variable = { name = zz_ef_cur value = flag:dollar_caribbean_dollar }
		zz_ef_cur_name_set = yes
	}
}

				name = circulating_dollar_sierra_leonean_dollar_c_var_1
				add = { 50000009 100000009 }
			}
			scope:central_bank_site ?= {
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = stockpiling_dollar_sierra_leonean_dollar_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_dollar_sierra_leonean_dollar_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = stockpiling_dollar_sierra_leonean_dollar_state_1
					add  = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_dollar_sierra_leonean_dollar_c_var_1
						multiply = 0.101
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = silver_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = silver_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = silver_state_1
					add = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_dollar_sierra_leonean_dollar_c_var_1
						add = var:stockpiling_dollar_sierra_leonean_dollar_state_1
                        add = owner.building_saving
						multiply = owner.var:money_value_target_1
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = gold_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = gold_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = gold_state_1
					add = {
						value = var:silver_state_1
						multiply = silver_to_gold_rate
					}
				}
			}

			#Event new central bank and new currency
			if = {
				limit = {
					not = {
						has_variable = country_already_central_bank
					}
				}
				set_variable = country_already_central_bank

				if = {
					limit = { exists = capital }
					capital= {
						save_scope_as = capital_state
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = target_country

				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_player = yes
					}
					trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.1
				}
			}

		}
		if = { #Gold standart
			limit = {
				has_law = law_type:law_fiat_standard
				game_date > 1873.1.1
			}

			activate_law = law_type:law_gold_standard

			if = {
				limit = { exists = capital }
				capital = {
					save_scope_as = central_bank_site
				}
			}

			set_variable = {
				name = money_value_target_1
				value = { 1 2 }
			}
			if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = circulating_dollar_sierra_leonean_dollar_c_var_1 } } set_variable = { name = circulating_dollar_sierra_leonean_dollar_c_var_1 value = 0 } }
			change_variable = {
				name = circulating_dollar_sierra_leonean_dollar_c_var_1
				add = { 50000009 100000009 }
			}
			scope:central_bank_site ?= {
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = stockpiling_dollar_sierra_leonean_dollar_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_dollar_sierra_leonean_dollar_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = stockpiling_dollar_sierra_leonean_dollar_state_1
					add  = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_dollar_sierra_leonean_dollar_c_var_1
						multiply = 0.101
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = gold_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = gold_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = gold_state_1
					add = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_dollar_sierra_leonean_dollar_c_var_1
						add = var:stockpiling_dollar_sierra_leonean_dollar_state_1
						add = owner.building_saving
						multiply = owner.var:money_value_target_1
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = silver_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = silver_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = silver_state_1
					add =  var:gold_state_1
				}
			}

			#Event new central bank and new currency
			if = {
				limit = {
					not = {
						has_variable = country_already_central_bank
					}
				}
				set_variable = country_already_central_bank

				if = {
					limit = { exists = capital }
					capital= {
						save_scope_as = capital_state
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = target_country

				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_player = yes
					}
					trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.1
				}
			}

		}
		set_variable = { name = zz_ef_cur value = flag:dollar_sierra_leonean_dollar }
		zz_ef_cur_name_set = yes
	}
}
#dollar_united_states_dollar
introduction_of_dollar_united_states_dollar = {
	if = {
		limit = { #Introduction of dollar_united_states_dollar
			currency_identifiers_dollar_united_states_dollar = 1
		}
		if = { #Silver standart

				game_date < 1873.1.1
			}

					save_scope_as = central_bank_site
				}
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = money_value_target_1
				value = { 15 30 } #{ 1 12 }
			}

					}
				}
			}

			#Event new central bank and new currency
			if = {
				limit = {
					not = {
						has_variable = country_already_central_bank
					}
				}
				set_variable = country_already_central_bank

				if = {
					limit = { exists = capital }
					capital= {
						save_scope_as = capital_state
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = target_country

				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_player = yes
					}
					trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.1
				}
			}

		}
		if = { #Gold standart
			limit = {
				has_law = law_type:law_fiat_standard
				game_date > 1873.1.1
			}

			activate_law = law_type:law_gold_standard

			if = {
				limit = { exists = capital }
				capital = {
					save_scope_as = central_bank_site
				}
			}

			set_variable = {
				name = money_value_target_1
				value = { 1 2 }
			}
			if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = circulating_eco_east_african_eco_c_var_1 } } set_variable = { name = circulating_eco_east_african_eco_c_var_1 value = 0 } }
			change_variable = {
				name = circulating_eco_east_african_eco_c_var_1
				add = { 50000009 100000009 }
			}
			scope:central_bank_site ?= {
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = stockpiling_eco_east_african_eco_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_eco_east_african_eco_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = stockpiling_eco_east_african_eco_state_1
					add  = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_eco_east_african_eco_c_var_1
						multiply = 0.101
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = gold_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = gold_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = gold_state_1
					add = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_eco_east_african_eco_c_var_1
						add = var:stockpiling_eco_east_african_eco_state_1
						add = owner.building_saving
						multiply = owner.var:money_value_target_1
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = silver_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = silver_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = silver_state_1
					add =  var:gold_state_1
				}
			}

			#Event new central bank and new currency
			if = {
				limit = {

				}

				if = {
					limit = { exists = capital }

						save_scope_as = capital_state
					}
				}

					}
					trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.1
				}
			}

		}
		set_variable = { name = zz_ef_cur value = flag:eco_east_african_eco }
		zz_ef_cur_name_set = yes
	}

			if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = circulating_eco_south_african_rand_c_var_1 } } set_variable = { name = circulating_eco_south_african_rand_c_var_1 value = 0 } }
			change_variable = {
				name = circulating_eco_south_african_rand_c_var_1
				add = { 50000009 100000009 }
			}
			scope:central_bank_site ?= {
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = stockpiling_eco_south_african_rand_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_eco_south_african_rand_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = stockpiling_eco_south_african_rand_state_1
					add  = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_eco_south_african_rand_c_var_1
						multiply = 0.101
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = gold_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = gold_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = gold_state_1
					add = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_eco_south_african_rand_c_var_1
						add = var:stockpiling_eco_south_african_rand_state_1
						add = owner.building_saving
						multiply = owner.var:money_value_target_1
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = silver_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = silver_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = silver_state_1
					add =  var:gold_state_1
				}
			}

			#Event new central bank and new currency
			if = {
				limit = {
					not = {
						has_variable = country_already_central_bank
					}
				}
				set_variable = country_already_central_bank

				if = {
					limit = { exists = capital }
					capital= {
						save_scope_as = capital_state
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = target_country

				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_player = yes
					}
					trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.1
				}
			}

		}
		set_variable = { name = zz_ef_cur value = flag:eco_south_african_rand }
		zz_ef_cur_name_set = yes
	}
}
#eco_tuareg_ouguiya
introduction_of_eco_tuareg_ouguiya = {
	if = {
		limit = { #Introduction of eco_tuareg_ouguiya
			currency_identifiers_eco_tuareg_ouguiya = 1
		}
		if = { #Silver standart
			limit = {
				has_law = law_type:law_fiat_standard
				game_date < 1873.1.1
			}

			activate_law = law_type:law_silver_standard

			if = {
				limit = { exists = capital }
				capital = {
					save_scope_as = central_bank_site
				}
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = money_value_target_1
				value = { 15 30 } #{ 1 12 }
			}
			if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = circulating_eco_tuareg_ouguiya_c_var_1 } } set_variable = { name = circulating_eco_tuareg_ouguiya_c_var_1 value = 0 } }
			change_variable = {
				name = circulating_eco_tuareg_ouguiya_c_var_1
				add = { 50000009 100000009 }
			}
			scope:central_bank_site ?= {
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = stockpiling_eco_tuareg_ouguiya_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_eco_tuareg_ouguiya_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = stockpiling_eco_tuareg_ouguiya_state_1
					add  = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_eco_tuareg_ouguiya_c_var_1
						multiply = 0.101
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = silver_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = silver_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = silver_state_1
					add = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_eco_tuareg_ouguiya_c_var_1
						add = var:stockpiling_eco_tuareg_ouguiya_state_1
                        add = owner.building_saving
						multiply = owner.var:money_value_target_1
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = gold_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = gold_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = gold_state_1
					add = {
						value = var:silver_state_1
						multiply = silver_to_gold_rate
					}
				}
			}

			#Event new central bank and new currency
			if = {
				limit = {
					not = {
						has_variable = country_already_central_bank
					}
				}
				set_variable = country_already_central_bank

				if = {
					limit = { exists = capital }
					capital= {
						save_scope_as = capital_state
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = target_country

				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_player = yes
					}
					trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.1
				}
			}

		}
		if = { #Gold standart
			limit = {
				has_law = law_type:law_fiat_standard
				game_date > 1873.1.1
			}

			activate_law = law_type:law_gold_standard

			if = {
				limit = { exists = capital }
				capital = {
					save_scope_as = central_bank_site
				}
			}

			set_variable = {
				name = money_value_target_1
				value = { 1 2 }
			}
			if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = circulating_eco_tuareg_ouguiya_c_var_1 } } set_variable = { name = circulating_eco_tuareg_ouguiya_c_var_1 value = 0 } }
			change_variable = {
				name = circulating_eco_tuareg_ouguiya_c_var_1
				add = { 50000009 100000009 }
			}
			scope:central_bank_site ?= {
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = stockpiling_eco_tuareg_ouguiya_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_eco_tuareg_ouguiya_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = stockpiling_eco_tuareg_ouguiya_state_1
					add  = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_eco_tuareg_ouguiya_c_var_1
						multiply = 0.101
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = gold_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = gold_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = gold_state_1
					add = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_eco_tuareg_ouguiya_c_var_1
						add = var:stockpiling_eco_tuareg_ouguiya_state_1
						add = owner.building_saving
						multiply = owner.var:money_value_target_1
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = silver_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = silver_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = silver_state_1
					add =  var:gold_state_1

					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = silver_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = silver_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = silver_state_1
					add =  var:gold_state_1
				}
			}

			#Event new central bank and new currency
			if = {
				limit = {
					not = {
						has_variable = country_already_central_bank
					}
				}
				set_variable = country_already_central_bank

				if = {
					limit = { exists = capital }
					capital= {
						save_scope_as = capital_state
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = target_country

				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_player = yes
					}
					trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.1
				}
			}

		}
		set_variable = { name = zz_ef_cur value = flag:franc_french_franc }
		zz_ef_cur_name_set = yes
	}
}
#franc_luxembourgish_franc
introduction_of_franc_luxembourgish_franc = {
	if = {
		limit = { #Introduction of franc_luxembourgish_franc
			currency_identifiers_franc_luxembourgish_franc = 1
		}
		if = { #Silver standart
			limit = {
				has_law = law_type:law_fiat_standard
				game_date < 1873.1.1
			}

			activate_law = law_type:law_silver_standard

			if = {
				limit = { exists = capital }
				capital = {
					save_scope_as = central_bank_site
				}
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = money_value_target_1
				value = { 15 30 } #{ 1 12 }
			}
			if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = circulating_franc_luxembourgish_franc_c_var_1 } } set_variable = { name = circulating_franc_luxembourgish_franc_c_var_1 value = 0 } }
			change_variable = {
				name = circulating_franc_luxembourgish_franc_c_var_1
				add = { 50000009 100000009 }
			}
			scope:central_bank_site ?= {
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = stockpiling_franc_luxembourgish_franc_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_franc_luxembourgish_franc_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = stockpiling_franc_luxembourgish_franc_state_1
					add  = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_franc_luxembourgish_franc_c_var_1
						multiply = 0.101
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = silver_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = silver_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = silver_state_1
					add = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_franc_luxembourgish_franc_c_var_1
						add = var:stockpiling_franc_luxembourgish_franc_state_1
                        add = owner.building_saving
						multiply = owner.var:money_value_target_1
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = gold_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = gold_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = gold_state_1
					add = {
						value = var:silver_state_1
						multiply = silver_to_gold_rate
					}
				}
			}

			#Event new central bank and new currency
			if = {
				limit = {
					not = {
						has_variable = country_already_central_bank
					}
				}
				set_variable = country_already_central_bank

				if = {
					limit = { exists = capital }
					capital= {
						save_scope_as = capital_state
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = target_country

				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_player = yes
					}
					trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.1
				}
			}

		}
		if = { #Gold standart
			limit = {
				has_law = law_type:law_fiat_standard
				game_date > 1873.1.1
			}

			activate_law = law_type:law_gold_standard

			if = {
				limit = { exists = capital }
				capital = {
					save_scope_as = central_bank_site
				}
			}

			set_variable = {
				name = money_value_target_1
				value = { 1 2 }
			}
			if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = circulating_franc_luxembourgish_franc_c_var_1 } } set_variable = { name = circulating_franc_luxembourgish_franc_c_var_1 value = 0 } }
			change_variable = {
				name = circulating_franc_luxembourgish_franc_c_var_1
				add = { 50000009 100000009 }
			}
			scope:central_bank_site ?= {
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = stockpiling_franc_luxembourgish_franc_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_franc_luxembourgish_franc_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = stockpiling_franc_luxembourgish_franc_state_1
					add  = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_franc_luxembourgish_franc_c_var_1
						multiply = 0.101
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = gold_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = gold_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = gold_state_1
					add = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_franc_luxembourgish_franc_c_var_1
						add = var:stockpiling_franc_luxembourgish_franc_state_1
						add = owner.building_saving
						multiply = owner.var:money_value_target_1
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = silver_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = silver_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = silver_state_1
					add =  var:gold_state_1
				}
			}

			#Event new central bank and new currency
			if = {
				limit = {
					not = {
						has_variable = country_already_central_bank
					}
				}
				set_variable = country_already_central_bank

				if = {
					limit = { exists = capital }
					capital= {
						save_scope_as = capital_state
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = target_country

				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_player = yes
					}
					trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.1
				}
			}

		}
		set_variable = { name = zz_ef_cur value = flag:franc_luxembourgish_franc }
		zz_ef_cur_name_set = yes
	}
}

				value = { 15 30 } #{ 1 12 }
			}
			if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = circulating_gulden_florin_c_var_1 } } set_variable = { name = circulating_gulden_florin_c_var_1 value = 0 } }
			change_variable = {
				name = circulating_gulden_florin_c_var_1
				add = { 50000009 100000009 }
			}
			scope:central_bank_site ?= {
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = stockpiling_gulden_florin_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_gulden_florin_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = stockpiling_gulden_florin_state_1
					add  = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_gulden_florin_c_var_1
						multiply = 0.101
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = silver_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = silver_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = silver_state_1
					add = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_gulden_florin_c_var_1
						add = var:stockpiling_gulden_florin_state_1
                        add = owner.building_saving
						multiply = owner.var:money_value_target_1
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = gold_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = gold_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = gold_state_1
					add = {
						value = var:silver_state_1
						multiply = silver_to_gold_rate
					}
				}
			}

			#Event new central bank and new currency
			if = {
				limit = {
					not = {
						has_variable = country_already_central_bank
					}
				}
				set_variable = country_already_central_bank

				if = {
					limit = { exists = capital }
					capital= {
						save_scope_as = capital_state
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = target_country

				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_player = yes
					}
					trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.1
				}
			}

		}
		if = { #Gold standart
			limit = {
				has_law = law_type:law_fiat_standard
				game_date > 1873.1.1
			}

			activate_law = law_type:law_gold_standard

			if = {
				limit = { exists = capital }
				capital = {
					save_scope_as = central_bank_site
				}
			}

			set_variable = {
				name = money_value_target_1
				value = { 1 2 }
			}
			if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = circulating_gulden_florin_c_var_1 } } set_variable = { name = circulating_gulden_florin_c_var_1 value = 0 } }
			change_variable = {
				name = circulating_gulden_florin_c_var_1
				add = { 50000009 100000009 }
			}
			scope:central_bank_site ?= {
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = stockpiling_gulden_florin_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_gulden_florin_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = stockpiling_gulden_florin_state_1
					add  = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_gulden_florin_c_var_1
						multiply = 0.101
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = gold_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = gold_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = gold_state_1
					add = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_gulden_florin_c_var_1
						add = var:stockpiling_gulden_florin_state_1
						add = owner.building_saving
						multiply = owner.var:money_value_target_1
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = silver_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = silver_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = silver_state_1
					add =  var:gold_state_1
				}
			}

			#Event new central bank and new currency
			if = {
				limit = {
					not = {
						has_variable = country_already_central_bank
					}
				}
				set_variable = country_already_central_bank

				if = {
					limit = { exists = capital }
					capital= {
						save_scope_as = capital_state
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = target_country

				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_player = yes
					}
					trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.1
				}
			}

		}
		set_variable = { name = zz_ef_cur value = flag:gulden_florin }
		zz_ef_cur_name_set = yes
	}
}

				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = gold_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = gold_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = gold_state_1
					add = {
						value = var:silver_state_1
						multiply = silver_to_gold_rate
					}
				}
			}

			#Event new central bank and new currency
			if = {
				limit = {
					not = {
						has_variable = country_already_central_bank
					}
				}
				set_variable = country_already_central_bank

				if = {
					limit = { exists = capital }
					capital= {
						save_scope_as = capital_state
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = target_country

				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_player = yes
					}
					trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.1
				}
			}

		}
		if = { #Gold standart
			limit = {
				has_law = law_type:law_fiat_standard
				game_date > 1873.1.1
			}

			activate_law = law_type:law_gold_standard

			if = {
				limit = { exists = capital }
				capital = {
					save_scope_as = central_bank_site
				}
			}

			set_variable = {
				name = money_value_target_1
				value = { 1 2 }
			}
			if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = circulating_krone_czech_koruna_c_var_1 } } set_variable = { name = circulating_krone_czech_koruna_c_var_1 value = 0 } }
			change_variable = {
				name = circulating_krone_czech_koruna_c_var_1
				add = { 50000009 100000009 }
			}
			scope:central_bank_site ?= {
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = stockpiling_krone_czech_koruna_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_krone_czech_koruna_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = stockpiling_krone_czech_koruna_state_1
					add  = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_krone_czech_koruna_c_var_1
						multiply = 0.101
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = gold_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = gold_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = gold_state_1
					add = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_krone_czech_koruna_c_var_1
						add = var:stockpiling_krone_czech_koruna_state_1
						add = owner.building_saving
						multiply = owner.var:money_value_target_1
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = silver_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = silver_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = silver_state_1
					add =  var:gold_state_1
				}
			}

			#Event new central bank and new currency

					}
					trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.1
				}
			}

		}
		set_variable = { name = zz_ef_cur value = flag:krone_czech_koruna }
		zz_ef_cur_name_set = yes
	}

#krone_danish_krone
introduction_of_krone_danish_krone = {
	if = {
		limit = { #Introduction of krone_danish_krone
			currency_identifiers_krone_danish_krone = 1
		}
		if = { #Silver standart
			limit = {
				has_law = law_type:law_fiat_standard
				game_date < 1873.1.1
			}

			activate_law = law_type:law_silver_standard

			if = {
				limit = { exists = capital }
				capital = {
					save_scope_as = central_bank_site
				}
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = money_value_target_1
				value = { 15 30 } #{ 1 12 }
			}
			if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = circulating_krone_danish_krone_c_var_1 } } set_variable = { name = circulating_krone_danish_krone_c_var_1 value = 0 } }
			change_variable = {
				name = circulating_krone_danish_krone_c_var_1
				add = { 50000009 100000009 }
			}
			scope:central_bank_site ?= {
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = stockpiling_krone_danish_krone_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_krone_danish_krone_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = stockpiling_krone_danish_krone_state_1
					add  = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_krone_danish_krone_c_var_1
						multiply = 0.101
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = silver_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = silver_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = silver_state_1
					add = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_krone_danish_krone_c_var_1
						add = var:stockpiling_krone_danish_krone_state_1
                        add = owner.building_saving
						multiply = owner.var:money_value_target_1
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = gold_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = gold_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = gold_state_1
					add = {
						value = var:silver_state_1

					}

			}

					not = {
						has_variable = country_already_central_bank
					}
				}
				set_variable = country_already_central_bank

				if = {
					limit = { exists = capital }
					capital= {
						save_scope_as = capital_state
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = target_country

				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_player = yes
					}
					trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.1
				}
			}

		}
		if = { #Gold standart
			limit = {
				has_law = law_type:law_fiat_standard
				game_date > 1873.1.1
			}

			activate_law = law_type:law_gold_standard

			if = {
				limit = { exists = capital }
				capital = {
					save_scope_as = central_bank_site
				}
			}

			set_variable = {
				name = money_value_target_1
				value = { 1 2 }
			}
			if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = circulating_krone_danish_krone_c_var_1 } } set_variable = { name = circulating_krone_danish_krone_c_var_1 value = 0 } }
			change_variable = {

			if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = circulating_krone_norwegian_krone_c_var_1 } } set_variable = { name = circulating_krone_norwegian_krone_c_var_1 value = 0 } }
			change_variable = {
				name = circulating_krone_norwegian_krone_c_var_1
				add = { 50000009 100000009 }
			}
			scope:central_bank_site ?= {
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = stockpiling_krone_norwegian_krone_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_krone_norwegian_krone_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = stockpiling_krone_norwegian_krone_state_1
					add  = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_krone_norwegian_krone_c_var_1
						multiply = 0.101
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = gold_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = gold_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = gold_state_1
					add = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_krone_norwegian_krone_c_var_1
						add = var:stockpiling_krone_norwegian_krone_state_1
						add = owner.building_saving
						multiply = owner.var:money_value_target_1
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = silver_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = silver_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = silver_state_1
					add =  var:gold_state_1
				}
			}

			#Event new central bank and new currency
			if = {
				limit = {
					not = {
						has_variable = country_already_central_bank
					}
				}
				set_variable = country_already_central_bank

				if = {
					limit = { exists = capital }
					capital= {
						save_scope_as = capital_state
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = target_country

				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_player = yes
					}
					trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.1
				}
			}

		}
		set_variable = { name = zz_ef_cur value = flag:krone_norwegian_krone }
		zz_ef_cur_name_set = yes
	}
}
#krone_slovak_koruna
introduction_of_krone_slovak_koruna = {
	if = {
		limit = { #Introduction of krone_slovak_koruna
			currency_identifiers_krone_slovak_koruna = 1
		}
		if = { #Silver standart
			limit = {
				has_law = law_type:law_fiat_standard
				game_date < 1873.1.1
			}

			activate_law = law_type:law_silver_standard

			if = {
				limit = { exists = capital }
				capital = {
					save_scope_as = central_bank_site
				}
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = money_value_target_1
				value = { 15 30 } #{ 1 12 }
			}
			if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = circulating_krone_slovak_koruna_c_var_1 } } set_variable = { name = circulating_krone_slovak_koruna_c_var_1 value = 0 } }
			change_variable = {
				name = circulating_krone_slovak_koruna_c_var_1
				add = { 50000009 100000009 }
			}
			scope:central_bank_site ?= {
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = stockpiling_krone_slovak_koruna_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_krone_slovak_koruna_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = stockpiling_krone_slovak_koruna_state_1
					add  = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_krone_slovak_koruna_c_var_1
						multiply = 0.101
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = silver_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = silver_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = silver_state_1
					add = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_krone_slovak_koruna_c_var_1
						add = var:stockpiling_krone_slovak_koruna_state_1
                        add = owner.building_saving
						multiply = owner.var:money_value_target_1
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = gold_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = gold_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = gold_state_1
					add = {
						value = var:silver_state_1
						multiply = silver_to_gold_rate
					}
				}
			}

			#Event new central bank and new currency
			if = {
				limit = {
					not = {
						has_variable = country_already_central_bank
					}
				}
				set_variable = country_already_central_bank

				if = {
					limit = { exists = capital }
					capital= {
						save_scope_as = capital_state
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = target_country

				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_player = yes
					}
					trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.1
				}
			}

		}
		if = { #Gold standart
			limit = {
				has_law = law_type:law_fiat_standard
				game_date > 1873.1.1
			}

			activate_law = law_type:law_gold_standard

			if = {
				limit = { exists = capital }
				capital = {
					save_scope_as = central_bank_site
				}
			}

			set_variable = {
				name = money_value_target_1
				value = { 1 2 }
			}
			if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = circulating_krone_slovak_koruna_c_var_1 } } set_variable = { name = circulating_krone_slovak_koruna_c_var_1 value = 0 } }
			change_variable = {
				name = circulating_krone_slovak_koruna_c_var_1
				add = { 50000009 100000009 }
			}
			scope:central_bank_site ?= {
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = stockpiling_krone_slovak_koruna_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_krone_slovak_koruna_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = stockpiling_krone_slovak_koruna_state_1
					add  = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_krone_slovak_koruna_c_var_1
						multiply = 0.101
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = gold_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = gold_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = gold_state_1
					add = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_krone_slovak_koruna_c_var_1
						add = var:stockpiling_krone_slovak_koruna_state_1
						add = owner.building_saving
						multiply = owner.var:money_value_target_1
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = silver_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = silver_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = silver_state_1
					add =  var:gold_state_1

						save_scope_as = capital_state
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = target_country

				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_player = yes
					}
					trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.1
				}
			}

		}
		set_variable = { name = zz_ef_cur value = flag:leon_lev }
		zz_ef_cur_name_set = yes
	}
}
#lira
introduction_of_lira = {
	if = {
		limit = { #Introduction of lira
			currency_identifiers_lira = 1
		}
		if = { #Silver standart
			limit = {
				has_law = law_type:law_fiat_standard
				game_date < 1873.1.1
			}

			activate_law = law_type:law_silver_standard

			if = {
				limit = { exists = capital }
				capital = {
					save_scope_as = central_bank_site
				}
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = money_value_target_1
				value = { 15 30 } #{ 1 12 }
			}
			if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = circulating_lira_c_var_1 } } set_variable = { name = circulating_lira_c_var_1 value = 0 } }
			change_variable = {
				name = circulating_lira_c_var_1
				add = { 50000009 100000009 }
			}
			scope:central_bank_site ?= {
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = stockpiling_lira_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_lira_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = stockpiling_lira_state_1
					add  = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_lira_c_var_1
						multiply = 0.101
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = silver_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = silver_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = silver_state_1
					add = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_lira_c_var_1
						add = var:stockpiling_lira_state_1
                        add = owner.building_saving
						multiply = owner.var:money_value_target_1
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = gold_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = gold_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = gold_state_1
					add = {
						value = var:silver_state_1
						multiply = silver_to_gold_rate
					}
				}
			}

			#Event new central bank and new currency
			if = {
				limit = {
					not = {
						has_variable = country_already_central_bank
					}
				}
				set_variable = country_already_central_bank

				if = {
					limit = { exists = capital }
					capital= {
						save_scope_as = capital_state
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = target_country

				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_player = yes
					}
					trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.1
				}
			}

		}
		if = { #Gold standart
			limit = {
				has_law = law_type:law_fiat_standard
				game_date > 1873.1.1
			}

			activate_law = law_type:law_gold_standard

			if = {
				limit = { exists = capital }
				capital = {
					save_scope_as = central_bank_site
				}
			}

			set_variable = {
				name = money_value_target_1
				value = { 1 2 }
			}
			if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = circulating_lira_c_var_1 } } set_variable = { name = circulating_lira_c_var_1 value = 0 } }
			change_variable = {
				name = circulating_lira_c_var_1
				add = { 50000009 100000009 }
			}
			scope:central_bank_site ?= {
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = stockpiling_lira_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_lira_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = stockpiling_lira_state_1
					add  = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_lira_c_var_1
						multiply = 0.101
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = gold_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = gold_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = gold_state_1
					add = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_lira_c_var_1
						add = var:stockpiling_lira_state_1
						add = owner.building_saving
						multiply = owner.var:money_value_target_1
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = silver_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = silver_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = silver_state_1
					add =  var:gold_state_1
				}
			}

			#Event new central bank and new currency
			if = {
				limit = {
					not = {
						has_variable = country_already_central_bank
					}
				}
				set_variable = country_already_central_bank

				if = {
					limit = { exists = capital }
					capital= {
						save_scope_as = capital_state
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = target_country

				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_player = yes
					}
					trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.1
				}
			}

		}
		set_variable = { name = zz_ef_cur value = flag:lira }
		zz_ef_cur_name_set = yes
	}
}

				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = silver_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = silver_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = silver_state_1
					add = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_lira_scudo_sardo_c_var_1
						add = var:stockpiling_lira_scudo_sardo_state_1
                        add = owner.building_saving
						multiply = owner.var:money_value_target_1
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = gold_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = gold_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = gold_state_1
					add = {
						value = var:silver_state_1
						multiply = silver_to_gold_rate
					}
				}
			}

			#Event new central bank and new currency
			if = {
				limit = {
					not = {
						has_variable = country_already_central_bank
					}
				}
				set_variable = country_already_central_bank

				if = {
					limit = { exists = capital }
					capital= {
						save_scope_as = capital_state
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = target_country

				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_player = yes
					}
					trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.1
				}
			}

		}
		if = { #Gold standart
			limit = {
				has_law = law_type:law_fiat_standard
				game_date > 1873.1.1
			}

			activate_law = law_type:law_gold_standard

			if = {
				limit = { exists = capital }
				capital = {
					save_scope_as = central_bank_site
				}
			}

			set_variable = {
				name = money_value_target_1
				value = { 1 2 }
			}
			if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = circulating_lira_scudo_sardo_c_var_1 } } set_variable = { name = circulating_lira_scudo_sardo_c_var_1 value = 0 } }
			change_variable = {
				name = circulating_lira_scudo_sardo_c_var_1
				add = { 50000009 100000009 }
			}
			scope:central_bank_site ?= {
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = stockpiling_lira_scudo_sardo_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_lira_scudo_sardo_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = stockpiling_lira_scudo_sardo_state_1
					add  = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_lira_scudo_sardo_c_var_1
						multiply = 0.101
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = gold_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = gold_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = gold_state_1
					add = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_lira_scudo_sardo_c_var_1
						add = var:stockpiling_lira_scudo_sardo_state_1
						add = owner.building_saving
						multiply = owner.var:money_value_target_1
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = silver_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = silver_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = silver_state_1
					add =  var:gold_state_1
				}
			}

			#Event new central bank and new currency
			if = {
				limit = {
					not = {
						has_variable = country_already_central_bank
					}
				}
				set_variable = country_already_central_bank

				if = {
					limit = { exists = capital }
					capital= {
						save_scope_as = capital_state
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = target_country

				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_player = yes
					}
					trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.1
				}
			}

		}
		set_variable = { name = zz_ef_cur value = flag:lira_scudo_sardo }
		zz_ef_cur_name_set = yes
	}
}
#lira_toscane_lira
introduction_of_lira_toscane_lira = {
	if = {
		limit = { #Introduction of lira_toscane_lira
			currency_identifiers_lira_toscane_lira = 1
		}
		if = { #Silver standart
			limit = {
				has_law = law_type:law_fiat_standard
				game_date < 1873.1.1
			}

			activate_law = law_type:law_silver_standard

			if = {
				limit = { exists = capital }
				capital = {
					save_scope_as = central_bank_site
				}
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = money_value_target_1
				value = { 15 30 } #{ 1 12 }
			}
			if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = circulating_lira_toscane_lira_c_var_1 } } set_variable = { name = circulating_lira_toscane_lira_c_var_1 value = 0 } }
			change_variable = {
				name = circulating_lira_toscane_lira_c_var_1
				add = { 50000009 100000009 }
			}
			scope:central_bank_site ?= {
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = stockpiling_lira_toscane_lira_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_lira_toscane_lira_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = stockpiling_lira_toscane_lira_state_1
					add  = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_lira_toscane_lira_c_var_1
						multiply = 0.101
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = silver_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = silver_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = silver_state_1
					add = {

						add = var:stockpiling_lira_toscane_lira_state_1

			scope:central_bank_site ?= {
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = stockpiling_peso_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_peso_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = stockpiling_peso_state_1
					add  = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_peso_c_var_1
						multiply = 0.101
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = gold_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = gold_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = gold_state_1
					add = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_peso_c_var_1
						add = var:stockpiling_peso_state_1
						add = owner.building_saving
						multiply = owner.var:money_value_target_1
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = silver_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = silver_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = silver_state_1
					add =  var:gold_state_1
				}
			}

			#Event new central bank and new currency
			if = {
				limit = {
					not = {
						has_variable = country_already_central_bank
					}
				}
				set_variable = country_already_central_bank

				if = {
					limit = { exists = capital }
					capital= {
						save_scope_as = capital_state
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = target_country

				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_player = yes
					}
					trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.1
				}
			}

		}
		set_variable = { name = zz_ef_cur value = flag:peso }
		zz_ef_cur_name_set = yes
	}
}
#peso_argentine_peso
introduction_of_peso_argentine_peso = {
	if = {
		limit = { #Introduction of peso_argentine_peso
			currency_identifiers_peso_argentine_peso = 1
		}
		if = { #Silver standart
			limit = {
				has_law = law_type:law_fiat_standard
				game_date < 1873.1.1
			}

			activate_law = law_type:law_silver_standard

			if = {
				limit = { exists = capital }
				capital = {
					save_scope_as = central_bank_site
				}
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = money_value_target_1
				value = { 15 30 } #{ 1 12 }
			}
			if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = circulating_peso_argentine_peso_c_var_1 } } set_variable = { name = circulating_peso_argentine_peso_c_var_1 value = 0 } }
			change_variable = {
				name = circulating_peso_argentine_peso_c_var_1
				add = { 50000009 100000009 }
			}
			scope:central_bank_site ?= {
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = stockpiling_peso_argentine_peso_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_peso_argentine_peso_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = stockpiling_peso_argentine_peso_state_1
					add  = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_peso_argentine_peso_c_var_1
						multiply = 0.101
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = silver_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = silver_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = silver_state_1
					add = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_peso_argentine_peso_c_var_1
						add = var:stockpiling_peso_argentine_peso_state_1
                        add = owner.building_saving
						multiply = owner.var:money_value_target_1
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = gold_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = gold_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = gold_state_1
					add = {
						value = var:silver_state_1
						multiply = silver_to_gold_rate
					}
				}
			}

			#Event new central bank and new currency
			if = {
				limit = {
					not = {
						has_variable = country_already_central_bank
					}
				}
				set_variable = country_already_central_bank

				if = {
					limit = { exists = capital }
					capital= {
						save_scope_as = capital_state
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = target_country

				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_player = yes
					}
					trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.1
				}
			}

		}
		if = { #Gold standart
			limit = {
				has_law = law_type:law_fiat_standard
				game_date > 1873.1.1
			}

			activate_law = law_type:law_gold_standard

			if = {
				limit = { exists = capital }
				capital = {
					save_scope_as = central_bank_site
				}
			}

			set_variable = {
				name = money_value_target_1
				value = { 1 2 }
			}
			if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = circulating_peso_argentine_peso_c_var_1 } } set_variable = { name = circulating_peso_argentine_peso_c_var_1 value = 0 } }
			change_variable = {
				name = circulating_peso_argentine_peso_c_var_1
				add = { 50000009 100000009 }
			}
			scope:central_bank_site ?= {
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = stockpiling_peso_argentine_peso_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_peso_argentine_peso_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = stockpiling_peso_argentine_peso_state_1
					add  = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_peso_argentine_peso_c_var_1
						multiply = 0.101
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = gold_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = gold_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = gold_state_1
					add = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_peso_argentine_peso_c_var_1
						add = var:stockpiling_peso_argentine_peso_state_1
						add = owner.building_saving
						multiply = owner.var:money_value_target_1
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = silver_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = silver_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = silver_state_1
					add =  var:gold_state_1
				}
			}

			#Event new central bank and new currency
			if = {
				limit = {
					not = {
						has_variable = country_already_central_bank
					}
				}
				set_variable = country_already_central_bank

				if = {
					limit = { exists = capital }
					capital= {
						save_scope_as = capital_state
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = target_country

				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_player = yes
					}
					trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.1
				}
			}

		}
		set_variable = { name = zz_ef_cur value = flag:peso_argentine_peso }
		zz_ef_cur_name_set = yes
	}
}

#peso_costa_rican_colon
introduction_of_peso_costa_rican_colon = {
	if = {
		limit = { #Introduction of peso_costa_rican_colon
			currency_identifiers_peso_costa_rican_colon = 1
		}
		if = { #Silver standart
			limit = {
				has_law = law_type:law_fiat_standard
				game_date < 1873.1.1
			}

			activate_law = law_type:law_silver_standard

			if = {
				limit = { exists = capital }
				capital = {
					save_scope_as = central_bank_site
				}
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = money_value_target_1
				value = { 15 30 } #{ 1 12 }
			}
			if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = circulating_peso_costa_rican_colon_c_var_1 } } set_variable = { name = circulating_peso_costa_rican_colon_c_var_1 value = 0 } }
			change_variable = {
				name = circulating_peso_costa_rican_colon_c_var_1
				add = { 50000009 100000009 }
			}
			scope:central_bank_site ?= {
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = stockpiling_peso_costa_rican_colon_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_peso_costa_rican_colon_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = stockpiling_peso_costa_rican_colon_state_1
					add  = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_peso_costa_rican_colon_c_var_1
						multiply = 0.101
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = silver_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = silver_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = silver_state_1
					add = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_peso_costa_rican_colon_c_var_1
						add = var:stockpiling_peso_costa_rican_colon_state_1
                        add = owner.building_saving
						multiply = owner.var:money_value_target_1
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = gold_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = gold_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = gold_state_1
					add = {
						value = var:silver_state_1
						multiply = silver_to_gold_rate
					}
				}
			}

			#Event new central bank and new currency
			if = {
				limit = {
					not = {
						has_variable = country_already_central_bank
					}
				}
				set_variable = country_already_central_bank

				if = {
					limit = { exists = capital }
					capital= {
						save_scope_as = capital_state
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = target_country

				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_player = yes
					}
					trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.1
				}
			}

		}
		if = { #Gold standart
			limit = {
				has_law = law_type:law_fiat_standard
				game_date > 1873.1.1
			}

			activate_law = law_type:law_gold_standard

			if = {
				limit = { exists = capital }
				capital = {
					save_scope_as = central_bank_site
				}
			}

			set_variable = {
				name = money_value_target_1
				value = { 1 2 }
			}
			if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = circulating_peso_costa_rican_colon_c_var_1 } } set_variable = { name = circulating_peso_costa_rican_colon_c_var_1 value = 0 } }
			change_variable = {
				name = circulating_peso_costa_rican_colon_c_var_1
				add = { 50000009 100000009 }
			}
			scope:central_bank_site ?= {
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = stockpiling_peso_costa_rican_colon_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_peso_costa_rican_colon_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = stockpiling_peso_costa_rican_colon_state_1
					add  = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_peso_costa_rican_colon_c_var_1
						multiply = 0.101
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = gold_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = gold_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = gold_state_1
					add = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_peso_costa_rican_colon_c_var_1
						add = var:stockpiling_peso_costa_rican_colon_state_1
						add = owner.building_saving
						multiply = owner.var:money_value_target_1
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = silver_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = silver_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = silver_state_1
					add =  var:gold_state_1
				}
			}

			#Event new central bank and new currency
			if = {
				limit = {
					not = {
						has_variable = country_already_central_bank
					}
				}
				set_variable = country_already_central_bank

				if = {
					limit = { exists = capital }
					capital= {
						save_scope_as = capital_state
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = target_country

				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_player = yes
					}
					trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.1
				}
			}

		}
		set_variable = { name = zz_ef_cur value = flag:peso_costa_rican_colon }
		zz_ef_cur_name_set = yes
	}
}

			if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = circulating_peso_guatemalan_quetzal_c_var_1 } } set_variable = { name = circulating_peso_guatemalan_quetzal_c_var_1 value = 0 } }
			change_variable = {
				name = circulating_peso_guatemalan_quetzal_c_var_1
				add = { 50000009 100000009 }
			}
			scope:central_bank_site ?= {
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = stockpiling_peso_guatemalan_quetzal_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_peso_guatemalan_quetzal_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = stockpiling_peso_guatemalan_quetzal_state_1
					add  = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_peso_guatemalan_quetzal_c_var_1
						multiply = 0.101
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = silver_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = silver_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = silver_state_1
					add = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_peso_guatemalan_quetzal_c_var_1
						add = var:stockpiling_peso_guatemalan_quetzal_state_1
                        add = owner.building_saving
						multiply = owner.var:money_value_target_1
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = gold_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = gold_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = gold_state_1
					add = {
						value = var:silver_state_1
						multiply = silver_to_gold_rate
					}
				}
			}

			#Event new central bank and new currency
			if = {
				limit = {
					not = {
						has_variable = country_already_central_bank
					}
				}
				set_variable = country_already_central_bank

				if = {
					limit = { exists = capital }
					capital= {
						save_scope_as = capital_state
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = target_country

				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_player = yes
					}
					trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.1
				}
			}

		}
		if = { #Gold standart
			limit = {
				has_law = law_type:law_fiat_standard
				game_date > 1873.1.1
			}

			activate_law = law_type:law_gold_standard

			if = {
				limit = { exists = capital }
				capital = {
					save_scope_as = central_bank_site
				}
			}

			set_variable = {
				name = money_value_target_1
				value = { 1 2 }
			}
			if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = circulating_peso_guatemalan_quetzal_c_var_1 } } set_variable = { name = circulating_peso_guatemalan_quetzal_c_var_1 value = 0 } }
			change_variable = {
				name = circulating_peso_guatemalan_quetzal_c_var_1
				add = { 50000009 100000009 }
			}
			scope:central_bank_site ?= {
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = stockpiling_peso_guatemalan_quetzal_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_peso_guatemalan_quetzal_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = stockpiling_peso_guatemalan_quetzal_state_1
					add  = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_peso_guatemalan_quetzal_c_var_1
						multiply = 0.101
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = gold_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = gold_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = gold_state_1
					add = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_peso_guatemalan_quetzal_c_var_1
						add = var:stockpiling_peso_guatemalan_quetzal_state_1
						add = owner.building_saving
						multiply = owner.var:money_value_target_1
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = silver_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = silver_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = silver_state_1
					add =  var:gold_state_1
				}
			}

			#Event new central bank and new currency
			if = {
				limit = {
					not = {
						has_variable = country_already_central_bank
					}
				}
				set_variable = country_already_central_bank

				if = {
					limit = { exists = capital }
					capital= {
						save_scope_as = capital_state
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = target_country

				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_player = yes
					}
					trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.1
				}
			}

		}
		set_variable = { name = zz_ef_cur value = flag:peso_guatemalan_quetzal }
		zz_ef_cur_name_set = yes
	}
}
