						add = var:stockpiling_peso_paraguayan_peso_state_1
                        add = owner.building_saving
						multiply = owner.var:money_value_target_1
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = gold_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = gold_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = gold_state_1
					add = {
						value = var:silver_state_1
						multiply = silver_to_gold_rate
					}
				}
			}

			#Event new central bank and new currency
			if = {
				limit = {
					not = {
						has_variable = country_already_central_bank
					}
				}
				set_variable = country_already_central_bank

				if = {
					limit = { exists = capital }
					capital= {
						save_scope_as = capital_state
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = target_country

				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_player = yes
					}
					trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.1
				}
			}

		}
		if = { #Gold standart
			limit = {
				has_law = law_type:law_fiat_standard
				game_date > 1873.1.1
			}

			activate_law = law_type:law_gold_standard

			if = {
				limit = { exists = capital }
				capital = {
					save_scope_as = central_bank_site
				}
			}

			set_variable = {
				name = money_value_target_1
				value = { 1 2 }
			}
			if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = circulating_peso_paraguayan_peso_c_var_1 } } set_variable = { name = circulating_peso_paraguayan_peso_c_var_1 value = 0 } }
			change_variable = {
				name = circulating_peso_paraguayan_peso_c_var_1
				add = { 50000009 100000009 }
			}
			scope:central_bank_site ?= {
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = stockpiling_peso_paraguayan_peso_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_peso_paraguayan_peso_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = stockpiling_peso_paraguayan_peso_state_1
					add  = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_peso_paraguayan_peso_c_var_1
						multiply = 0.101
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = gold_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = gold_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = gold_state_1
					add = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_peso_paraguayan_peso_c_var_1
						add = var:stockpiling_peso_paraguayan_peso_state_1
						add = owner.building_saving
						multiply = owner.var:money_value_target_1
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = silver_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = silver_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = silver_state_1
					add =  var:gold_state_1
				}
			}

			#Event new central bank and new currency
			if = {
				limit = {
					not = {
						has_variable = country_already_central_bank
					}
				}
				set_variable = country_already_central_bank

				if = {
					limit = { exists = capital }
					capital= {
						save_scope_as = capital_state
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = target_country

				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_player = yes
					}
					trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.1
				}
			}

		}
		set_variable = { name = zz_ef_cur value = flag:peso_paraguayan_peso }
		zz_ef_cur_name_set = yes
	}
}

				limit = { exists = capital }
				capital = {
					save_scope_as = central_bank_site
				}
			}

			set_variable = {
				name = money_value_target_1
				value = { 1 2 }
			}
			if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = circulating_peso_venezuelan_peso_c_var_1 } } set_variable = { name = circulating_peso_venezuelan_peso_c_var_1 value = 0 } }
			change_variable = {
				name = circulating_peso_venezuelan_peso_c_var_1
				add = { 50000009 100000009 }
			}
			scope:central_bank_site ?= {
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = stockpiling_peso_venezuelan_peso_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_peso_venezuelan_peso_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = stockpiling_peso_venezuelan_peso_state_1
					add  = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_peso_venezuelan_peso_c_var_1
						multiply = 0.101
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = gold_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = gold_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = gold_state_1
					add = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_peso_venezuelan_peso_c_var_1
						add = var:stockpiling_peso_venezuelan_peso_state_1
						add = owner.building_saving
						multiply = owner.var:money_value_target_1
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = silver_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = silver_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = silver_state_1
					add =  var:gold_state_1
				}
			}

			#Event new central bank and new currency
			if = {
				limit = {
					not = {
						has_variable = country_already_central_bank
					}
				}
				set_variable = country_already_central_bank

				if = {
					limit = { exists = capital }
					capital= {
						save_scope_as = capital_state
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = target_country

				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_player = yes
					}
					trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.1
				}
			}

		}
		set_variable = { name = zz_ef_cur value = flag:peso_venezuelan_peso }
		zz_ef_cur_name_set = yes
	}
}
#pound_egyptian_pound
introduction_of_pound_egyptian_pound = {
	if = {
		limit = { #Introduction of pound_egyptian_pound
			currency_identifiers_pound_egyptian_pound = 1
		}
		if = { #Silver standart
			limit = {
				has_law = law_type:law_fiat_standard
				game_date < 1873.1.1
			}

			activate_law = law_type:law_silver_standard

			if = {
				limit = { exists = capital }
				capital = {
					save_scope_as = central_bank_site
				}
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = money_value_target_1
				value = { 15 30 } #{ 1 12 }
			}
			if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = circulating_pound_egyptian_pound_c_var_1 } } set_variable = { name = circulating_pound_egyptian_pound_c_var_1 value = 0 } }
			change_variable = {
				name = circulating_pound_egyptian_pound_c_var_1
				add = { 50000009 100000009 }
			}
			scope:central_bank_site ?= {
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = stockpiling_pound_egyptian_pound_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_pound_egyptian_pound_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = stockpiling_pound_egyptian_pound_state_1
					add  = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_pound_egyptian_pound_c_var_1
						multiply = 0.101
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = silver_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = silver_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = silver_state_1
					add = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_pound_egyptian_pound_c_var_1
						add = var:stockpiling_pound_egyptian_pound_state_1
                        add = owner.building_saving
						multiply = owner.var:money_value_target_1
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = gold_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = gold_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = gold_state_1
					add = {
						value = var:silver_state_1
						multiply = silver_to_gold_rate
					}
				}
			}

			#Event new central bank and new currency
			if = {
				limit = {
					not = {
						has_variable = country_already_central_bank
					}
				}
				set_variable = country_already_central_bank

				if = {
					limit = { exists = capital }
					capital= {
						save_scope_as = capital_state
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = target_country

				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_player = yes
					}
					trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.1
				}
			}

		}
		if = { #Gold standart
			limit = {
				has_law = law_type:law_fiat_standard
				game_date > 1873.1.1
			}

			activate_law = law_type:law_gold_standard

			if = {
				limit = { exists = capital }
				capital = {
					save_scope_as = central_bank_site
				}
			}

			set_variable = {
				name = money_value_target_1
				value = { 1 2 }
			}
			if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = circulating_pound_egyptian_pound_c_var_1 } } set_variable = { name = circulating_pound_egyptian_pound_c_var_1 value = 0 } }
			change_variable = {
				name = circulating_pound_egyptian_pound_c_var_1
				add = { 50000009 100000009 }
			}
			scope:central_bank_site ?= {
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = stockpiling_pound_egyptian_pound_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_pound_egyptian_pound_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = stockpiling_pound_egyptian_pound_state_1
					add  = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_pound_egyptian_pound_c_var_1
						multiply = 0.101
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = gold_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = gold_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = gold_state_1
					add = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_pound_egyptian_pound_c_var_1
						add = var:stockpiling_pound_egyptian_pound_state_1
						add = owner.building_saving
						multiply = owner.var:money_value_target_1
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = silver_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = silver_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = silver_state_1
					add =  var:gold_state_1
				}
			}

			#Event new central bank and new currency
			if = {
				limit = {
					not = {
						has_variable = country_already_central_bank
					}
				}
				set_variable = country_already_central_bank

				if = {
					limit = { exists = capital }
					capital= {
						save_scope_as = capital_state
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = target_country

				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_player = yes
					}
					trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.1
				}
			}

		}
		set_variable = { name = zz_ef_cur value = flag:pound_egyptian_pound }
		zz_ef_cur_name_set = yes
	}
}

		zz_ef_cur_name_set = yes
	}
}
#real_brazilian_real
introduction_of_real_brazilian_real = {
	if = {
		limit = { #Introduction of real_brazilian_real
			currency_identifiers_real_brazilian_real = 1
		}
		if = { #Silver standart
			limit = {
				has_law = law_type:law_fiat_standard
				game_date < 1873.1.1
			}

			activate_law = law_type:law_silver_standard

			if = {
				limit = { exists = capital }
				capital = {
					save_scope_as = central_bank_site
				}
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = money_value_target_1
				value = { 15 30 } #{ 1 12 }
			}
			if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = circulating_real_brazilian_real_c_var_1 } } set_variable = { name = circulating_real_brazilian_real_c_var_1 value = 0 } }
			change_variable = {
				name = circulating_real_brazilian_real_c_var_1
				add = { 50000009 100000009 }
			}
			scope:central_bank_site ?= {
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = stockpiling_real_brazilian_real_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_real_brazilian_real_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = stockpiling_real_brazilian_real_state_1
					add  = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_real_brazilian_real_c_var_1
						multiply = 0.101
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = silver_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = silver_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = silver_state_1
					add = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_real_brazilian_real_c_var_1
						add = var:stockpiling_real_brazilian_real_state_1
                        add = owner.building_saving
						multiply = owner.var:money_value_target_1
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = gold_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = gold_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = gold_state_1
					add = {
						value = var:silver_state_1
						multiply = silver_to_gold_rate
					}
				}
			}

			#Event new central bank and new currency
			if = {
				limit = {
					not = {
						has_variable = country_already_central_bank
					}
				}
				set_variable = country_already_central_bank

				if = {
					limit = { exists = capital }
					capital= {
						save_scope_as = capital_state
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = target_country

				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_player = yes
					}
					trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.1
				}
			}

		}
		if = { #Gold standart
			limit = {
				has_law = law_type:law_fiat_standard
				game_date > 1873.1.1
			}

			activate_law = law_type:law_gold_standard

			if = {
				limit = { exists = capital }
				capital = {
					save_scope_as = central_bank_site
				}
			}

			set_variable = {
				name = money_value_target_1
				value = { 1 2 }
			}
			if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = circulating_real_brazilian_real_c_var_1 } } set_variable = { name = circulating_real_brazilian_real_c_var_1 value = 0 } }
			change_variable = {
				name = circulating_real_brazilian_real_c_var_1
				add = { 50000009 100000009 }
			}
			scope:central_bank_site ?= {
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = stockpiling_real_brazilian_real_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_real_brazilian_real_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = stockpiling_real_brazilian_real_state_1
					add  = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_real_brazilian_real_c_var_1
						multiply = 0.101
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = gold_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = gold_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = gold_state_1
					add = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_real_brazilian_real_c_var_1
						add = var:stockpiling_real_brazilian_real_state_1
						add = owner.building_saving
						multiply = owner.var:money_value_target_1
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = silver_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = silver_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = silver_state_1
					add =  var:gold_state_1
				}
			}

			#Event new central bank and new currency
			if = {
				limit = {
					not = {
						has_variable = country_already_central_bank
					}
				}
				set_variable = country_already_central_bank

				if = {
					limit = { exists = capital }
					capital= {
						save_scope_as = capital_state
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = target_country

				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_player = yes
					}
					trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.1
				}
			}

		}
		set_variable = { name = zz_ef_cur value = flag:real_brazilian_real }
		zz_ef_cur_name_set = yes
	}
}

					}

				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = gold_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = gold_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = gold_state_1
					add = {
						value = var:silver_state_1
						multiply = silver_to_gold_rate
					}
				}
			}

			#Event new central bank and new currency
			if = {
				limit = {
					not = {
						has_variable = country_already_central_bank
					}
				}
				set_variable = country_already_central_bank

				if = {
					limit = { exists = capital }
					capital= {
						save_scope_as = capital_state
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = target_country

				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_player = yes
					}
					trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.1
				}
			}

		}
		if = { #Gold standart
			limit = {
				has_law = law_type:law_fiat_standard
				game_date > 1873.1.1
			}

			activate_law = law_type:law_gold_standard

			if = {
				limit = { exists = capital }
				capital = {
					save_scope_as = central_bank_site
				}
			}

			set_variable = {
				name = money_value_target_1
				value = { 1 2 }
			}
			if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = circulating_spe_dong_c_var_1 } } set_variable = { name = circulating_spe_dong_c_var_1 value = 0 } }
			change_variable = {
				name = circulating_spe_dong_c_var_1
				add = { 50000009 100000009 }
			}
			scope:central_bank_site ?= {
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = stockpiling_spe_dong_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_spe_dong_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = stockpiling_spe_dong_state_1
					add  = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_spe_dong_c_var_1
						multiply = 0.101
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = gold_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = gold_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = gold_state_1
					add = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_spe_dong_c_var_1
						add = var:stockpiling_spe_dong_state_1
						add = owner.building_saving
						multiply = owner.var:money_value_target_1
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = silver_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = silver_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = silver_state_1
					add =  var:gold_state_1
				}
			}

			#Event new central bank and new currency
			if = {
				limit = {
					not = {
						has_variable = country_already_central_bank
					}
				}
				set_variable = country_already_central_bank

				if = {
					limit = { exists = capital }
					capital= {
						save_scope_as = capital_state
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = target_country

				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_player = yes
					}
					trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.1
				}
			}

		}
		set_variable = { name = zz_ef_cur value = flag:spe_dong }
		zz_ef_cur_name_set = yes
	}
}
#spe_drachma
introduction_of_spe_drachma = {
	if = {
		limit = { #Introduction of spe_drachma
			currency_identifiers_spe_drachma = 1
		}
		if = { #Silver standart
			limit = {
				has_law = law_type:law_fiat_standard
				game_date < 1873.1.1
			}

			activate_law = law_type:law_silver_standard

			if = {
				limit = { exists = capital }
				capital = {
					save_scope_as = central_bank_site
				}
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = money_value_target_1
				value = { 15 30 } #{ 1 12 }
			}
			if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = circulating_spe_drachma_c_var_1 } } set_variable = { name = circulating_spe_drachma_c_var_1 value = 0 } }
			change_variable = {
				name = circulating_spe_drachma_c_var_1
				add = { 50000009 100000009 }
			}
			scope:central_bank_site ?= {
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = stockpiling_spe_drachma_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_spe_drachma_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = stockpiling_spe_drachma_state_1
					add  = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_spe_drachma_c_var_1
						multiply = 0.101
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = silver_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = silver_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = silver_state_1
					add = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_spe_drachma_c_var_1
						add = var:stockpiling_spe_drachma_state_1
                        add = owner.building_saving
						multiply = owner.var:money_value_target_1
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = gold_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = gold_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = gold_state_1
					add = {
						value = var:silver_state_1
						multiply = silver_to_gold_rate
					}
				}
			}

					not = {
						has_variable = country_already_central_bank
					}
				}
				set_variable = country_already_central_bank

				if = {
					limit = { exists = capital }
					capital= {
						save_scope_as = capital_state
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = target_country

				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_player = yes
					}
					trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.1
				}
			}

		}
		if = { #Gold standart
			limit = {
				has_law = law_type:law_fiat_standard
				game_date > 1873.1.1
			}

			activate_law = law_type:law_gold_standard

			if = {
				limit = { exists = capital }
				capital = {
					save_scope_as = central_bank_site
				}
			}

			set_variable = {
				name = money_value_target_1
				value = { 1 2 }
			}
			if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = circulating_spe_drachma_c_var_1 } } set_variable = { name = circulating_spe_drachma_c_var_1 value = 0 } }
			change_variable = {
				name = circulating_spe_drachma_c_var_1
				add = { 50000009 100000009 }

			scope:central_bank_site ?= {
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = stockpiling_spe_drachma_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_spe_drachma_state_1 value = 0 } }

					name = stockpiling_spe_lithuanian_litas_state_1

						value = owner.var:circulating_spe_lithuanian_litas_c_var_1
						multiply = 0.101
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = gold_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = gold_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = gold_state_1
					add = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_spe_lithuanian_litas_c_var_1
						add = var:stockpiling_spe_lithuanian_litas_state_1
						add = owner.building_saving
						multiply = owner.var:money_value_target_1
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = silver_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = silver_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = silver_state_1
					add =  var:gold_state_1
				}
			}

			#Event new central bank and new currency
			if = {
				limit = {
					not = {
						has_variable = country_already_central_bank
					}
				}
				set_variable = country_already_central_bank

				if = {
					limit = { exists = capital }
					capital= {
						save_scope_as = capital_state
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = target_country

				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_player = yes
					}
					trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.1
				}
			}

		}
		set_variable = { name = zz_ef_cur value = flag:spe_lithuanian_litas }
		zz_ef_cur_name_set = yes
	}
}
#spe_peseta
introduction_of_spe_peseta = {
	if = {
		limit = { #Introduction of spe_peseta
			currency_identifiers_spe_peseta = 1
		}
		if = { #Silver standart
			limit = {
				has_law = law_type:law_fiat_standard
				game_date < 1873.1.1
			}

			activate_law = law_type:law_silver_standard

			if = {
				limit = { exists = capital }
				capital = {
					save_scope_as = central_bank_site
				}
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = money_value_target_1
				value = { 15 30 } #{ 1 12 }
			}
			if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = circulating_spe_peseta_c_var_1 } } set_variable = { name = circulating_spe_peseta_c_var_1 value = 0 } }
			change_variable = {
				name = circulating_spe_peseta_c_var_1
				add = { 50000009 100000009 }
			}
			scope:central_bank_site ?= {
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = stockpiling_spe_peseta_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_spe_peseta_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = stockpiling_spe_peseta_state_1
					add  = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_spe_peseta_c_var_1
						multiply = 0.101
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = silver_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = silver_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = silver_state_1
					add = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_spe_peseta_c_var_1
						add = var:stockpiling_spe_peseta_state_1
                        add = owner.building_saving
						multiply = owner.var:money_value_target_1
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = gold_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = gold_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = gold_state_1
					add = {
						value = var:silver_state_1
						multiply = silver_to_gold_rate
					}
				}
			}

			#Event new central bank and new currency
			if = {
				limit = {
					not = {
						has_variable = country_already_central_bank
					}
				}
				set_variable = country_already_central_bank

				if = {
					limit = { exists = capital }
					capital= {
						save_scope_as = capital_state
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = target_country

				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_player = yes
					}
					trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.1
				}
			}

		}
		if = { #Gold standart
			limit = {
				has_law = law_type:law_fiat_standard
				game_date > 1873.1.1
			}

			activate_law = law_type:law_gold_standard

			if = {
				limit = { exists = capital }
				capital = {
					save_scope_as = central_bank_site
				}
			}

			set_variable = {
				name = money_value_target_1
				value = { 1 2 }
			}
			if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = circulating_spe_peseta_c_var_1 } } set_variable = { name = circulating_spe_peseta_c_var_1 value = 0 } }
			change_variable = {
				name = circulating_spe_peseta_c_var_1
				add = { 50000009 100000009 }
			}
			scope:central_bank_site ?= {
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = stockpiling_spe_peseta_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_spe_peseta_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = stockpiling_spe_peseta_state_1
					add  = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_spe_peseta_c_var_1
						multiply = 0.101
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = gold_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = gold_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = gold_state_1
					add = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_spe_peseta_c_var_1
						add = var:stockpiling_spe_peseta_state_1
						add = owner.building_saving
						multiply = owner.var:money_value_target_1
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = silver_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = silver_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = silver_state_1
					add =  var:gold_state_1
				}
			}

			#Event new central bank and new currency
			if = {
				limit = {
					not = {
						has_variable = country_already_central_bank
					}
				}
				set_variable = country_already_central_bank

				if = {
					limit = { exists = capital }
					capital= {
						save_scope_as = capital_state
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = target_country

				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_player = yes
					}
					trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.1
				}
			}

		}
		set_variable = { name = zz_ef_cur value = flag:spe_peseta }
		zz_ef_cur_name_set = yes
	}
}

			}
			scope:central_bank_site ?= {
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = stockpiling_spe_yuan_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_spe_yuan_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = stockpiling_spe_yuan_state_1
					add  = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_spe_yuan_c_var_1
						multiply = 0.101
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = silver_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = silver_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = silver_state_1
					add = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_spe_yuan_c_var_1
						add = var:stockpiling_spe_yuan_state_1
                        add = owner.building_saving
						multiply = owner.var:money_value_target_1
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = gold_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = gold_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = gold_state_1
					add = {
						value = var:silver_state_1
						multiply = silver_to_gold_rate
					}
				}
			}

			#Event new central bank and new currency
			if = {
				limit = {
					not = {
						has_variable = country_already_central_bank
					}
				}
				set_variable = country_already_central_bank

				if = {
					limit = { exists = capital }
					capital= {
						save_scope_as = capital_state
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = target_country

				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_player = yes
					}
					trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.1
				}
			}

		}
		if = { #Gold standart
			limit = {
				has_law = law_type:law_fiat_standard
				game_date > 1873.1.1
			}

			activate_law = law_type:law_gold_standard

			if = {
				limit = { exists = capital }
				capital = {
					save_scope_as = central_bank_site
				}
			}

			set_variable = {
				name = money_value_target_1
				value = { 1 2 }
			}
			if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = circulating_spe_yuan_c_var_1 } } set_variable = { name = circulating_spe_yuan_c_var_1 value = 0 } }
			change_variable = {
				name = circulating_spe_yuan_c_var_1
				add = { 50000009 100000009 }
			}
			scope:central_bank_site ?= {
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = stockpiling_spe_yuan_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_spe_yuan_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = stockpiling_spe_yuan_state_1
					add  = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_spe_yuan_c_var_1
						multiply = 0.101
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = gold_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = gold_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = gold_state_1
					add = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_spe_yuan_c_var_1
						add = var:stockpiling_spe_yuan_state_1
						add = owner.building_saving
						multiply = owner.var:money_value_target_1
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = silver_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = silver_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = silver_state_1
					add =  var:gold_state_1
				}
			}

			#Event new central bank and new currency
			if = {
				limit = {
					not = {
						has_variable = country_already_central_bank
					}
				}
				set_variable = country_already_central_bank

				if = {
					limit = { exists = capital }
					capital= {
						save_scope_as = capital_state
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = target_country

				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_player = yes
					}
					trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.1
				}
			}

		}
		set_variable = { name = zz_ef_cur value = flag:spe_yuan }
		zz_ef_cur_name_set = yes
	}
}
#spe_zloti
introduction_of_spe_zloti = {
	if = {
		limit = { #Introduction of spe_zloti
			currency_identifiers_spe_zloti = 1
		}
		if = { #Silver standart
			limit = {
				has_law = law_type:law_fiat_standard
				game_date < 1873.1.1
			}

				}

						is_player = yes
					}
					trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.1
				}
			}

		}
		if = { #Gold standart
			limit = {
				has_law = law_type:law_fiat_standard
				game_date > 1873.1.1
			}

			activate_law = law_type:law_gold_standard

			if = {
				limit = { exists = capital }
				capital = {
					save_scope_as = central_bank_site
				}
			}

			set_variable = {
				name = money_value_target_1
				value = { 1 2 }
			}
			if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = circulating_thaler_saxon_thaler_c_var_1 } } set_variable = { name = circulating_thaler_saxon_thaler_c_var_1 value = 0 } }
			change_variable = {
				name = circulating_thaler_saxon_thaler_c_var_1
				add = { 50000009 100000009 }
			}
			scope:central_bank_site ?= {
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = stockpiling_thaler_saxon_thaler_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = stockpiling_thaler_saxon_thaler_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = stockpiling_thaler_saxon_thaler_state_1
					add  = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_thaler_saxon_thaler_c_var_1
						multiply = 0.101
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = gold_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = gold_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = gold_state_1
					add = {
						value = owner.var:circulating_thaler_saxon_thaler_c_var_1
						add = var:stockpiling_thaler_saxon_thaler_state_1
						add = owner.building_saving
						multiply = owner.var:money_value_target_1
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { not = { has_variable = silver_state_1 } } set_variable = { name = silver_state_1 value = 0 } }
				change_variable = {
					name = silver_state_1
					add =  var:gold_state_1
				}
			}

			#Event new central bank and new currency
			if = {
				limit = {
					not = {
						has_variable = country_already_central_bank
					}
				}
				set_variable = country_already_central_bank

				if = {
					limit = { exists = capital }
					capital= {
						save_scope_as = capital_state
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = target_country

				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_player = yes
					}
					trigger_event = 00_ef_economic_event.1
				}
			}

		}
		set_variable = { name = zz_ef_cur value = flag:thaler_saxon_thaler }
		zz_ef_cur_name_set = yes
	}
}
